	title = cosmo_estateadvisors_events.3000.t
	desc = cosmo_estateadvisors_events.3000.d
	picture = {
	trigger = {
	OR = {	
		any_ally = {
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = cosmo_requesting_advisors
			}
			primary_culture = cara
			}
		 any_subject_country = {
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = cosmo_requesting_advisors
			}
			primary_culture = cara			
			}			
		}	
	}

	}

				who = ROOT
				modifier = cosmo_requesting_advisors

			primary_culture = guajiro
			}

			has_opinion_modifier = {

			}

	}

		ai = no

				modifier = cosmo_requesting_advisors

			primary_culture = guajiro
			primary_culture = cara			
			}
			}
		 any_subject_country = {
			has_opinion_modifier = {
				who = ROOT
				modifier = cosmo_requesting_advisors
			}
		OR = {	
			primary_culture = muisca
			primary_culture = guajiro
			primary_culture = cara			
			}
			}			
		}
	}

			days = 1
		}

		name = cosmo_estateadvisors_events.3000.a
